        var u = navigator.userAgent;
        if(u.indexOf('Android') > -1 || u.indexOf('Linux') > -1){
          if(ourtoken){
             $('#appBtn').css('display','none')
          }else{
          	$('#appBtn').css('display','flex')
          }
        }
      })
      // 关闭弹框
      function cancel(){
        $('#appBtn').css('display','none')
        sessionStorage.setItem("closeFlag", true);
      }

      function submitFn(){
        //判断浏览器
        var u = navigator.userAgent;
        if(/MicroMessenger/gi.test(u) ){
          // 引导用户在浏览器中打开
          alert('请在浏览器中打开');
          return;
        }
        var d = new Date();
        var t0 = d.getTime();
        if(u.indexOf('Android') > -1 || u.indexOf('Linux') > -1){
          console.log("android")
          //Android
//           if(openApp('cupshe://home/path')){
//             openApp('cupshe://home/path');
//           }else{
            //由于打开需要1～2秒，利用这个时间差来处理－－打开app后，返回h5页面会出现页面变成app下载页面，影响用户体验
            window.location.href = 'cupshe://home/path'
            var delay = setInterval(function(){
              var d = new Date();
              var t1 = d.getTime();
              if( t1-t0<3000 && t1-t0>1500){
                // alert('请下载APP');
                window.location.href = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.cupshe.cupshe";
              }
              if(t1-t0>=3000){
                clearInterval(delay);
              }
            },1500);
//           }
        }
      }
      function openApp(src) {
        // 通过iframe的方式试图打开APP，如果能正常打开，会直接切换到APP，并自动阻止a标签的默认行为
        // 否则打开a标签的href链接
        var ifr = document.createElement('iframe');
        ifr.src = src;
        ifr.style.display = 'none';
        document.body.appendChild(ifr);
        window.setTimeout(function(){
          document.body.removeChild(ifr);
        },2000);
      }
    </script>

<script>
//   二、三级导航 右上角显示文字
  $(function(){
//     三级
    $('a.Collapsible__Button.Heading.Text--subdued.Link.Link--primary').each(function(index){
//       console.log(222,index,$(this).html())
      var text = $(this).html().split("&nbsp;");     
      var text1 = text[0];
      var text2 = text[1]
//       console.log(222,text,text1,text2)
      
      if(text.length > 1){
         $(this).text('');
      	 $(this).append('<div class="asup sup-parent">'+text1+'<div class="sub-text">'+text2+'</div></div>')
      }
     
    })
    
//     二级
    $('.Collapsible__Content li .Link--primary').each(function(index){
//       console.log(333,index,$(this).html())
      var text = $(this).html().split("&nbsp;");     
      var text1 = text[0];
      var text2 = text[1]
//       console.log(222,text,text1,text2)
      
      if(text.length > 1){
         $(this).text(text1);
      	 $(this).append('<div class="sub-text">'+text2+'</div>')
         $(this).addClass('sup-parent')
      }
     
    })
//    二级导航时button按钮时
     $('.Collapsible button.Collapsible__Button.u-h7').each(function(index){
//       console.log(444,index,$(this).html())
      var textP = $(this).html().split("<");    
      var text = textP[0].split('&nbsp;')
      var text1 = text[0];
      var text2 = text[1];
      var text3 = '<' + textP[1] + '<' + textP[2]
      console.log(55,text,text1,text2,text3)
      if(text.length > 1){
       	 $(this).html(text3);
         
      	 $(this).prepend('<a class="sup-parent Collapsible__Button">'+text1+'<div class="sub-text">'+text2+'</div></a>')
      }
     
    })
//  初始化导航
     
     initMenu();

  })
  
   function initMenu(){
  	 setTimeout(function(){ 
         $('.SidebarMenu__Nav>.Collapsible').each(function(index){
            var flag = $(this).children('button').attr('aria-expanded');
            if(flag == 'true'){
               $(this).addClass('selectActived')
            }else{
              $(this).removeClass('selectActived')
            }

         })
         
      }, 300);
  }
  
  
  function selectMemu(me){
  	var flag = $(me).children('button').attr('aria-expanded');
  	 if(flag == 'false'){
       setTimeout(function(){ 
         $(me).addClass('selectActived')
         $(me).siblings().removeClass('selectActived')
       }, 300);
     }
    if(flag == 'true'){
      setTimeout(function(){ 
        $(me).removeClass('selectActived')
      }, 300);
    }
    
    initMenu();
    
  }
  
  
//   二级导航右上小字结束
</script>

<script>
          function sku_formatData1(data){
                      var sku_all =data;
                      var sku_format = sku_all.match(/[a-zA-Z]+[0-9]+/);
                      sku_format = sku_format.toString();
                      return sku_format
           }    

            function addMinCartSubmit(){  
              snaptr('track','START_CHECKOUT');
              var data_ga=[];
              $(".get_ga_data").each(function(){
              
              	 data_ga.push({
//                    "id": sku_formatData1($(this).attr('ga_id')),
                "id": $(this).attr('ga_id').toString(),

                    "name": $(this).attr('ga_name'),
                    "list_name":$(this).attr('ga_list_name'),
                    "brand": "",
                    "category": "",
                    "variant": $(this).attr('ga_variant'),
                    "quantity":$(this).parent().find("input").val(),
                    "list_position": $(this).attr('ga_list_position'),
                    "price": $(this).attr('ga_price'),                    
                  })

              })
              
              
         			gtag('event', 'begin_checkout', {
                         "items": data_ga
//                       [ 
////                          ]
                        });

                        gtag("event", "checkout", { 
                        "event_category" : "checkout::min cart", 
                        "event_label" : "begin checkout"
                        });
            }
          </script>

<script>
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--announcement-bar-height', document.getElementById('shopify-section-announcement').offsetHeight + 'px');
       function show(){
			//获取目的日期
        var myyear=2020;
			var mymonth=5-1;
			var myday=1;
			var myhour=13;
			var myminute=13;
			var mysecond=13;
			var time=Number(new Date(myyear,mymonth,myday,myhour,myminute,mysecond));
			// var time=new Date(myyear,mymonth,myday,myhour,myminute,mysecond).getTime();
			//获取当前时间
			var nowTime=Date.now();
			// var nowTime=new Date().getTime();
			//获取时间差
			var timediff=Math.round((time-nowTime)/1000);
			//获取还剩多少天
			var day=parseInt(timediff/3600/24);
			//获取还剩多少小时
			var hour=parseInt(timediff/3600%24)+day*24;
        
			//获取还剩多少分钟
			var minute=parseInt(timediff/60%60);
           
			//获取还剩多少秒
			var second=timediff%60;
            if(second<10){
            second="0"+second
            }
			//输出还剩多少时间
// 			document.getElementById("1").innerHTML=day;
			document.getElementById("2").innerHTML=hour;
			document.getElementById("3").innerHTML=minute;
			document.getElementById("4").innerHTML=second;
			
			if(timediff>0){
            setTimeout(show,1000);
           
            }else{
            document.getElementById("2").innerHTML='0';
			document.getElementById("3").innerHTML='0';
			document.getElementById("4").innerHTML='0';
            
            }
        		
			}
      
      
      $(function(){
          show()
      })
    </script></div>

            'event_category':'header',
            'event_label':'logo'
          });

      })
      
      $(".Header__SecondaryNav .Heading").click(function(){
        var text=$(this).attr("href")
        var realText=''
        if(text=="/account/login"){
        	realText="account"
        }else if(text=="/search"){
        	realText="search"
        }else if(text=="/cart"){
       		 realText="cart"
        }
          gtag('event', 'click', { 
            'event_category':'header',
            'event_label':realText
          });

      })
      
    
    })
  
  </script>
</header>

         var databg =  $(this).find(".yotpo-image").attr("src")
         gtag('event', 'Instagram', { 
           'event_category':'homepage',
           'event_label':"img::"+databg
         });
         //     new ins ga in Popup
         var productName = $(".yotpo-lightbox .yotpo-lightbox-product-name")[0].innerHTML;
         var upBoolean = $(".yotpo-lightbox  .yotpo-icon-thumbs-up").parent().hasClass("voted");
         var downBoolean = $(".yotpo-lightbox  .yotpo-icon-thumbs-down").parent().hasClass("voted");
		//previous          
         $(".yotpo-lightbox-wrapper").on("click",".yotpo-icon-left-arrow-thin",function(){
           gtag('event', 'previous', { 
              'event_category':'imagePopup',
              'event_label':productName
            });
           productName = $(".yotpo-lightbox .yotpo-lightbox-product-name")[0].innerHTML;
           upBoolean = $(".yotpo-lightbox  .yotpo-icon-thumbs-up").parent().hasClass("voted");
           downBoolean = $(".yotpo-lightbox  .yotpo-icon-thumbs-down").parent().hasClass("voted");
         })
         //next
         $(".yotpo-lightbox-wrapper").on("click",".yotpo-icon-right-arrow-thin",function(){
           gtag('event', 'next', { 
              'event_category':'imagePopup',
              'event_label':productName
            });
           productName = $(".yotpo-lightbox .yotpo-lightbox-product-name")[0].innerHTML;

         })

              'event_category':'imagePopup',
              'event_label':productName
            });

              'event_label':productName
            });

         })

<div class="backTop" id="backTop" onclick="gtag('event', 'top', { 
                      'event_category':'footer',
                      'event_label':'top'})">
<img alt="" class="backTopImgM" src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0784/0207/t/74/assets/topM.png?4092" width="100"/>
<img alt="" class="backTopImgP1" src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0784/0207/t/74/assets/top-1.png?4092" width="100"/>
<img alt="" class="backTopImgP2" src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0784/0207/t/74/assets/top-2.png?4092" style="display:none" width="100"/>
</div>
<script>
      $("#backTop").click(function() {
          $("html, body").animate({
              scrollTop: $("html, body").offset().top }, {duration: 400,easing: "swing"});
          return false;
      });

        $(".backTopImgP1").mouseover(function(){
         	$(".backTopImgP1").css("display","none");
            $(".backTopImgP2").css("display","block");
        });
        $(".backTopImgP2").mouseout(function(){
          $(".backTopImgP1").css("display","block");
            $(".backTopImgP2").css("display","none");
        });
    
      window.onscroll = function(e){
          var e =e || window.event;
          var scrolltop=document.documentElement.scrollTop||document.body.scrollTop;
          if(scrolltop>300){
              $("#backTop").fadeIn(200)
          }else {
              $("#backTop").fadeOut(200)
          }
      }
  </script>
